906 entries, task-capture-clarify to task-execute. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **task-capture-clarify**: Collect commitments from email, chat, meetings, documents, calls and ideas into one inbox, then process each item with the Getting Things Done decisions: delete, do now under two minutes, delegate, defer to the calendar, create a project or file as reference, extracting actions, owners and dates from notes and threads. Use whenever someone wants a brain dump, to process their inbox or to-do list, to turn meeting notes, email threads or chat into tasks, or feels overwhelmed by loose commitments. ([afeezadebayo/planning-task-management-skills](https://github.com/afeezadebayo/planning-task-management-skills/tree/HEAD/task-capture-clarify))
- **task-capture-note**: Atomic note capture into the vault (or to the user's note system if Obsidian / Notion native connection is set). Tags by theme + source. - **task-capture-system**: Builds a complete task capture system with inbox location, capture criteria, two-minute rule, and processing cadence. Produces a deployable system specification the user can immediately adopt. Use when the user asks about capturing tasks, organizing incoming work, setting up a task inbox, or building a system to track everything they need to do. Do NOT use for prioritizing existing tasks (use task-prioritization), scheduling tasks into time blocks (use time-blocking), or enterprise project intake processes (use business project-management skills). Use when you read or write a card, check its AC lines, need a person's answer, report where a task stands, or hit something only the platform can fix.
